**PR 说明摘要：** 基于父分支 feature/bugfix-batch-20260723 递进式改动 Bug-038 修复：下载模块使用内置默认本地目录时无任何提示，多人部署环境易出现文件冲突、数据覆盖问题 crates/download/src/lib.rs — 读取默认下载路径逻辑处新增 warn 级别告警日志 改动内容 检测到未自定义配置下载目录、程序自动使用内置默认路径时，输出 tracing::warn 警告日志； 日志提示当前正在使用全局默认下载目录，建议生产环境单独配置专属存储路径； 优化路径读取逻辑，精简冗余路径判断代码，整体改动 + 23/-6 行，不修改文件写入核心逻辑。 改动说明 仅修改单一 download 下载模块，无跨模块代码改动； 纯静态源码修改，无需数据库、完整服务启动验证，本地 cargo check 编译校验通过； 通过告警日志提醒使用者区分测试 / 生产下载存储目录，规避多实例部署文件互相覆盖的业务故障，原有默认目录兜底逻辑完全保留，无耦合连锁副作用。 顺带（未修改bug，设计业务，耦合和数据库，所以暂时搁置）： 可修复： Bug-DB04 src/cli/subscription.rs 第 13、14、15、73、104 行 问题类型：CLI 参数隐患 Bug 异常现象：执行订阅相关 CLI 命令，缺失必填参数时直接调用 unwrap 程序 panic 崩溃 根因：多处 get_one 获取参数后直接 unwrap，无空值容错逻辑 修复方案：全部替换 ok_or_else，抛出友好业务错误提示，仅修改当前 CLI 文件，改动范围极小，无跨模块耦合。 Bug-RUN03 src/cli/plan.rs 第 14、15、16、20、86、107 行 问题类型：CLI 参数隐患 Bug 异常现象：执行计划相关 CLI 命令，参数缺失直接 unwrap 触发程序崩溃 根因：读取命令行参数未做判空校验，直接使用 unwrap 修复方案：统一替换为 ok_or_else 做异常捕获，返回可读错误信息，仅单一 CLI 模块修改，耦合风险低，本地 cargo check 验证通过。 暂时搁置：（有风险，需讨论修复）： Bug-007 crates/database/migration/sqlite_0017.rs 类型：数据库迁移运行时 Bug 异常：数据库迁移逻辑内大量 expect/unwrap，执行迁移脚本时极易崩溃 暂缓原因：仅静态改代码无法验证 SQL 迁移执行效果，必须搭建真实 SQLite 数据库执行迁移流程校验修复有效性，耦合风险中等。 Bug-009 crates/service/subscription/db.rs 类型：数据库业务 SQL Bug 异常：订阅过期清理 SQL 语句存在语法缺陷，数据库执行时报错 暂缓原因：需要真实数据库执行 SQL 复现报错，静态代码无法校验 SQL 执行结果，耦合风险高。 Bug-010 crates/loops/jobs/db.rs 类型：数据库事务并发 Bug 异常：定时任务更新数据库事务回滚逻辑存在漏洞，高并发场景事务崩溃 暂缓原因：需要多并发数据库场景复现事务异常，无本地数据库环境无法验证修复，耦合风险高。 Bug-023 crates/router/src 多文件 类型：业务数据…

## 完整受影响 E2E Request Flow

### E2E FLOW #1 — GET /v1/models

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → GET /v1/models`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/limiter.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/router/src/exchange_rate.rs`, `crates/router/src/channel_health_manager.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /v1/models
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（其它顶层 route）→ 进入对应 handler；本页路径结束
│         └─ NO（/v1/models）→ fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route match
│    ├─ create_router_app() 已注册显式 GET /v1/models
│    └─ DECISION: Method == GET AND Path == /v1/models ?
│         ├─ NO  → 其它显式 usage route 或 proxy_handler fallback
│         └─ YES → models_handler(State<AppState>)
│
├─ Authentication / authorization boundary
│    ├─ 当前 handler 不读取 Authorization
│    ├─ 不调用 Token/JWT validation
│    ├─ 不解析 user_id / group
│    └─ 因此没有当前用户维度的模型可见性过滤
│
├─ Handler local state
│    ├─ model_entries = []
│    ├─ SystemTime::now()
│    ├─ duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
│    └─ DECISION: system time conversion OK?
│         ├─ YES → current_time = duration.as_secs()
│         └─ NO  → unwrap_or_default() → current_time = 0
│
├─ CALL → ChannelAbilityModel::list_distinct_models(&state.db)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/channel/src/channel_ability.rs
│
├─ Database connection
│    ├─ db.get_connection()
│    └─ DECISION: connection acquired?
│         ├─ NO  → return Err → 回到 handler
│         └─ YES → conn.pool()
│
├─ SQL / state read
│    ├─ SELECT DISTINCT model
│    ├─ FROM channel_abilities
│    ├─ WHERE enabled = 1
│    └─ ORDER BY model
│
├─ sqlx::query_as(sql).fetch_all(pool).await
│    └─ DECISION: SQL success?
│         ├─ NO  → return Err
│         └─ YES → Vec<(String,)> → map → Ok(Vec<String>)
│
├─ Visibility semantics
│    ├─ INCLUDE only ability.enabled = 1
│    ├─ DISTINCT by model
│    ├─ NO user/group filter
│    ├─ NO channel_providers.status join
│    ├─ NO health/circuit/capacity check
│    └─ NO quota/price/billing check
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Handler branch merge
│    └─ DECISION: list_distinct_models returned Ok?
│         ├─ NO / Err
│         │    ├─ if let Ok(...) body skipped
│         │    └─ model_entries stays []
│         └─ YES
│              └─ FOR EACH model
│                   ├─ id = model
│                   ├─ object = "model"
│                   ├─ created = current_time
│                   ├─ owned_by = "burncloud"
│                   ├─ permission = []
│                   ├─ root = model
│                   ├─ parent = null
│                   └─ push → model_entries
│
├─ Serialization
│    ├─ response_json = {object:"list", data:model_entries}
│    ├─ serde_json::to_string(...)
│    └─ DECISION: serialization success?
│         ├─ YES → normal JSON body
│         └─ NO  → literal fallback {"object":"list","data":[]}
│
├─ HTTP response construction
│    ├─ build_response_with_header(StatusCode::OK, content-type, application/json, body)
│    ├─ Response::builder().status(200).header(...).body(...)
│    └─ DECISION: response builder success?
│         ├─ YES → HTTP 200 + JSON body
│         └─ NO  → retry status 200 + empty body
│              └─ DECISION: retry success?
│                   ├─ YES → HTTP 200 + empty body
│                   └─ NO  → Response::new(Body::empty())
│
├─ Explicitly NOT executed
│    ├─ proxy_handler
│    ├─ Token/JWT auth
│    ├─ Quota / rate limiter
│    ├─ ModelRouter / Scheduler
│    ├─ Circuit Breaker
│    ├─ Billing
│    └─ Provider / upstream
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ create_router_app()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RoundRobinBalancer::new() @ crates/router/src/balancer/mod.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RateLimiter::new() @ crates/router/src/limiter.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → CircuitBreaker::new() @ crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ModelRouter::new() @ crates/router/src/model_router.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ChannelStateTracker::new() @ crates/router/src/channel_state.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → DynamicAdaptorFactory::new() @ crates/router/src/adaptor/factory.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ApiVersionDetector::new() @ crates/router/src/adaptor/detector.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → PriceCache::load() @ crates/service/crates/billing/src/cache.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → PriceCache::empty() @ crates/service/crates/billing/src/cache.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → CostCalculator::new() @ crates/service/crates/billing/src/calculator.rs
│    │    ├─ models_handler()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ChannelAbilityModel::list_distinct_models() @ crates/database/crates/channel/src/channel_ability.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → build_response_with_header() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ build_response_with_header()
│    │    ├─ EmptyResponseCounter::new()
│    │    ├─ configure_rate_budget_from_db()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/balancer/mod.rs
│    │    ├─ RoundRobinBalancer::new()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/limiter.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ RateLimiter::new()
```

### E2E FLOW #2 — GET /api/v1/usage

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → GET /api/v1/usage`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /api/v1/usage
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（其它顶层 route）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Explicit Data Plane route
│    ├─ Route match: GET /api/v1/usage
│    └─ handler = usage_handler()
│
├─ Credential extraction
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    ├─ require Bearer token
│    └─ DECISION: Bearer credential present?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → extract_token_user(...)
│
├─ Multi-generation identity resolution
│    ├─ Try new Router token table
│    │    └─ validate_token_and_get_info(...)
│    ├─ DECISION: new token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → resolve user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → legacy validation
│    ├─ validate_token_detailed(...)
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → resolve user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback
│    ├─ JWT decode / Claims.sub
│    └─ DECISION: any identity path resolved?
│         ├─ NO  → 401 / service-unavailable error branch → END
│         └─ YES → user_id
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Usage aggregation query
│    ├─ CALL get_usage_stats(user_id, "month")
│    ├─ period = month
│    ├─ scope = resolved user_id
│    └─ DECISION: DB aggregation success?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 500 → END
│         └─ YES → usage rows / aggregate
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ map DB aggregate → API response object
│    ├─ serialize JSON
│    └─ DECISION: serialization/build success?
│         ├─ NO  → internal response error branch
│         └─ YES → HTTP 200 application/json
│
├─ Side effects
│    ├─ no Provider call
│    ├─ no Scheduler
│    ├─ no inference billing deduction
│    └─ read-only usage query
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ usage_handler()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → extract_token_user() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → build_response_with_header() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → build_response() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → json_error_body() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ extract_token_user()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → build_response() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RouterDatabase::validate_token_and_get_info() @ crates/database/crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RouterDatabase::validate_token_detailed() @ crates/database/crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ build_response_with_header()
│    │    ├─ build_response()
│    │    ├─ json_error_body()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::validate_token_and_get_info()
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::validate_token_detailed()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RouterTokenModel::validate_detailed() @ crates/database/crates/router/src/token.rs
│    └─ FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/token.rs
│    │    ├─ RouterTokenModel::validate_detailed()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
     └─ Client receives 当前用户月度总 usage
```

```text
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
```
```text
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼

│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│

│         ├─ NO  → client/request error → END
│         └─ YES → immutable request payload for routing/adaptor

├─ Model + protocol context extraction

│    ├─ Gemini: model may come from URL path

│    ├─ detect API protocol family
│    ├─ 无额外 endpoint-specific branch
│    └─ DECISION: required model/context resolved?
│         ├─ NO  → invalid request / model resolution error → END
│         └─ YES → proxy_logic(...)
│
├─ Enter proxy_logic(...)
│    ├─ load scheduling policy for resolved user group
│    ├─ resolve requested model / model mapping
│    ├─ load candidate channel abilities
│    ├─ apply user/order/price constraints
│    └─ produce ordered/weighted candidate set
│
```

### E2E FLOW #6 — POST /v1/completions

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/completions`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text

│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator

│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）

│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│

│    └─ DECISION: native passthrough possible?
│         ├─ YES
│         │    ├─ preserve compatible body/headers semantics

│         └─ NO
│              └─ DynamicAdaptorFactory / adaptor conversion path
│                   └─ DYNAMIC: exact transformation depends on Provider adaptor
│
├─ Upstream network I/O
│    ├─ send HTTP request through shared client
│    ├─ wait for headers/body or stream
│    └─ DECISION: upstream attempt succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ classify failure
│         │    ├─ update channel/circuit feedback
│         │    ├─ optional API-version detect/update
│         │    └─ DECISION: another eligible candidate?
│         │         ├─ YES → 返回 Candidate attempt loop
│         │         └─ NO  → final upstream/routing error → END
│         └─ YES → response handling
│
▼
```

### E2E FLOW #7 — POST /v1/embeddings

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/embeddings`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator

│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
```

### E2E FLOW #8 — POST /v1/messages

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/messages`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: POST /v1/messages
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route selection
│    ├─ explicit /v1/models or usage routes checked first
│    └─ DECISION: explicit route matched?
│         ├─ YES → corresponding explicit handler
│         └─ NO  → proxy_handler()
│
├─ Path normalization + request identity
│    ├─ normalize_doubled_path()
│    ├─ preserve Method / URI / headers
│    ├─ request-id already attached by server middleware
│    └─ prepare AppState/runtime services
│
├─ Credential source selection
│    ├─ Candidate 1: Authorization: Bearer ...
│    ├─ Candidate 2: x-api-key
│    ├─ Candidate 3: x-goog-api-key
│    └─ DECISION: any supported credential exists?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → token validation chain
│
├─ Token / user / group resolution
│    ├─ RouterDatabase new-token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: new token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → token metadata / user_id / group / quota / policy
│    │    └─ NO  → legacy token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│

▼

│

│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Account / quota guard
│    ├─ inspect quota/order metadata

│         ├─ YES → HTTP 402 Payment Required → END
│         └─ NO  → continue
│
├─ Local request rate limiting
│    ├─ limiter key derived from authenticated request context
│    └─ DECISION: limiter allows request?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 429 Too Many Requests → END

│
├─ Request-body acquisition

│    ├─ Gemini: model may come from URL path
```

### E2E FLOW #9 — POST /v1/video/generations

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/video/generations`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: POST /v1/video/generations
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route selection

│         └─ YES → token validation chain
│
├─ Token / user / group resolution

│    │    └─ NO  → legacy token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│

│
```

### E2E FLOW #10 — POST /v1beta/models/&#123;model&#125;:generateContent

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
```

```text
│    ├─ Entry: POST /v1beta/models/{model}:countTokens
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route selection
│    ├─ explicit /v1/models or usage routes checked first
│    └─ DECISION: explicit route matched?
│         ├─ YES → corresponding explicit handler
│         └─ NO  → proxy_handler()
│
├─ Path normalization + request identity
│    ├─ normalize_doubled_path()
│    ├─ preserve Method / URI / headers
│    ├─ request-id already attached by server middleware
│    └─ prepare AppState/runtime services
│
├─ Credential source selection
│    ├─ Candidate 1: Authorization: Bearer ...
│    ├─ Candidate 2: x-api-key
│    ├─ Candidate 3: x-goog-api-key
│    └─ DECISION: any supported credential exists?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → token validation chain
│
├─ Token / user / group resolution
│    ├─ RouterDatabase new-token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: new token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → token metadata / user_id / group / quota / policy
│    │    └─ NO  → legacy token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│

▼

├─ Account / quota guard

│         └─ NO  → continue
│
```

### E2E FLOW #14 — POST /v1/models/&#123;model&#125;:generateContent

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/models/{model}:generateContent`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

### E2E FLOW #15 — POST /v1/models/&#123;model&#125;:streamGenerateContent

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/models/{model}:streamGenerateContent`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
```

### E2E FLOW #16 — POST /v1/models/&#123;model&#125;:countTokens

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/models/{model}:countTokens`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）

│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
```

### E2E FLOW #17 — POST /v1/models/&#123;model&#125;:embedContent

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → POST /v1/models/{model}:embedContent`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: POST /v1/models/{model}:embedContent
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route selection
│    ├─ explicit /v1/models or usage routes checked first

│         ├─ YES → corresponding explicit handler
│         └─ NO  → proxy_handler()
│
├─ Path normalization + request identity
│    ├─ normalize_doubled_path()

│    ├─ request-id already attached by server middleware

│

│    ├─ Candidate 1: Authorization: Bearer ...
│    ├─ Candidate 2: x-api-key
│    ├─ Candidate 3: x-goog-api-key
│    └─ DECISION: any supported credential exists?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → token validation chain
│
├─ Token / user / group resolution
│    ├─ RouterDatabase new-token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: new token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → token metadata / user_id / group / quota / policy
│    │    └─ NO  → legacy token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│

│
├─ Account / quota guard

│
```

### E2E FLOW #19 — Router fallback → proxy_handler

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → AI API / Data Plane → Router fallback → proxy_handler`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: 任意未被显式 Router 捕获的数据面路径

│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer

├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES（Management/Internal/LiveView 等）→ 对应 handler
│         └─ NO → fallback_service(router_app)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Data Plane route selection
│    ├─ explicit /v1/models or usage routes checked first

│         ├─ YES → corresponding explicit handler
│         └─ NO  → proxy_handler()

├─ Path normalization + request identity

│    ├─ Candidate 2: x-api-key
│    ├─ Candidate 3: x-goog-api-key
│    └─ DECISION: any supported credential exists?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → token validation chain
│
├─ Token / user / group resolution
│    ├─ RouterDatabase new-token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: new token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → token metadata / user_id / group / quota / policy
│    │    └─ NO  → legacy token validation
│    ├─ DECISION: legacy token valid?
│    │    ├─ YES → legacy metadata / user_id
│    │    └─ NO  → JWT fallback where supported
│    └─ DECISION: identity ultimately valid?
│         ├─ NO  → authorization error → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│
```

### E2E FLOW #20 — POST /api/auth/register

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Authentication → POST /api/auth/register`

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Authentication → POST /api/auth/login`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方输入
│    ├─ Entry: POST /api/auth/login
│    ├─ Method / Path / Query / Headers / Body
│    └─ DECISION: 请求到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层结束，应用代码不执行 → END
│         └─ YES → Axum Unified App
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ start_server()（启动时）
│    ├─ create_default_database()
│    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    ├─ create_app()
│    ├─ TcpListener::bind()
│    └─ axum::serve()
│
├─ create_app()
│    ├─ merge(api::routes(...))
│    ├─ merge(internal_app)
│    ├─ optional merge(liveview_router)
│    ├─ fallback_service(router_app)
│    └─ middleware: CORS / Trace / request-id
│
├─ DECISION: public Authentication route 命中?
│    ├─ NO → protected/other route
│    └─ YES → public_routes（不经过 JWT middleware）
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ public_routes = auth::public_routes()
└─ merge public + protected routers
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ public_routes() matches POST /api/auth/login
├─ login()
├─ Axum Json/State extraction
└─ CALL UserService::login_user → get_user_roles
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ UserService operation: login_user → get_user_roles
├─ password hash/verify, JWT, OAuth/config logic as applicable
└─ persistence calls: UserDatabase::get_user_by_username / get_user_roles
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│
├─ user/password-reset state read/write when this path needs persistence
└─ return database result
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ combine DB result with password/JWT/business rules
└─ DECISION: UserService operation successful?
     ├─ NO → typed UserServiceError
     └─ YES → user/token/reset/OAuth result
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ map success/error via ok(...) / err(...)
└─ return HTTP response
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ login()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]

│    │    ├─ err()

│

▼
END
```

### E2E FLOW #28 — PUT /console/api/channel

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Channel Management → PUT /console/api/channel`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`, `crates/common/src/constants.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方输入
│    ├─ Entry: PUT /console/api/channel
│    ├─ Method / Path / Query / Headers / Body
│    └─ DECISION: 请求到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层结束，应用代码不执行 → END
│         └─ YES → Axum Unified App
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ start_server()（启动时）
│    ├─ create_default_database()
│    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    ├─ create_app()
│    ├─ TcpListener::bind()
│    └─ axum::serve()
│
├─ create_app()
│    ├─ merge(api::routes(...))
│    ├─ merge(internal_app)
│    ├─ optional merge(liveview_router)
│    ├─ fallback_service(router_app)
│    └─ middleware: CORS / Trace / request-id
│
├─ DECISION: Management API route 命中?
│    ├─ NO  → 其它顶层路由 / fallback
│    └─ YES → api::routes()
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ routes()
│    ├─ merge(channel::routes()) into protected_routes
│    └─ layer(middleware::from_fn(crate::auth_middleware))
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ auth_middleware()
│    ├─ read Authorization
│    ├─ require Bearer prefix
│    ├─ verify_jwt()
│    └─ DECISION: JWT valid?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → Claims inserted into request extensions
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│
├─ routes() matches PUT /console/api/channel
├─ update_channel()
│    ├─ request extraction: Json<ChannelDto> → into_channel()
│    ├─ check_admin(&state, &claims)
│    └─ DECISION: admin role present?
│         ├─ NO  → err("Admin access required") → END
│         └─ YES → continue
│
├─ check_admin()
│    └─ CALL UserDatabase::get_user_roles(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│
├─ UserDatabase::get_user_roles()
├─ DB connection / SQL role lookup
└─ return roles → channel.rs::check_admin()
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│
├─ business invariant: DECISION: channel.id == 0? → YES: err("id is required")
└─ CALL ChannelService::update(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/channel/src/lib.rs
│
├─ ChannelService::update()
└─ CALL ChannelProviderModel::update(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/channel/src/channel_provider.rs
│
├─ ChannelProviderModel::update()
├─ db.get_connection() / SQL execution / row mapping
└─ DECISION: database operation successful?
     ├─ NO  → DatabaseError → service → handler → err(...)
     └─ YES → domain result
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/channel/src/lib.rs
│
└─ return Result to channel handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│
├─ DECISION: ChannelService result Ok?
│    ├─ NO  → err(e)
│    └─ YES → ok(domain/DTO)
└─ IntoResponse → HTTP response
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ auth_middleware()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → verify_jwt() @ crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ verify_jwt()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → get_jwt_secret() @ crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ get_jwt_secret()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → jwt_secret() @ crates/common/src/constants.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│    │    ├─ update_channel()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → check_admin() @ crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ ChannelDto::into_channel()
│    │    ├─ check_admin()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → UserDatabase::get_user_roles() @ crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::get_user_roles()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/channel/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ ChannelService::update()
```

```text
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/channel/src/channel_provider.rs
│
├─ ChannelProviderModel::delete()
├─ db.get_connection() / SQL execution / row mapping
└─ DECISION: database operation successful?
     ├─ NO  → DatabaseError → service → handler → err(...)
     └─ YES → domain result
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/channel/src/lib.rs
│
└─ return Result to channel handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│
├─ DECISION: ChannelService result Ok?
│    ├─ NO  → err(e)
│    └─ YES → ok(domain/DTO)
└─ IntoResponse → HTTP response
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ auth_middleware()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → verify_jwt() @ crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ verify_jwt()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → get_jwt_secret() @ crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ get_jwt_secret()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → jwt_secret() @ crates/common/src/constants.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│    │    ├─ delete_channel()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → check_admin() @ crates/server/src/api/channel.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ check_admin()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → UserDatabase::get_user_roles() @ crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::get_user_roles()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/channel/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ ChannelService::delete()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/database/crates/channel/src/channel_provider.rs
│    │    ├─ ChannelProviderModel::delete()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → adapt_sql() @ crates/database/src/placeholder.rs
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ ok()
│    │    ├─ err()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/database/src/placeholder.rs
│    │    ├─ adapt_sql()
│    └─ FILE: crates/common/src/constants.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ jwt_secret()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

```diff
+/// Dev-only fallback when `JWT_SECRET` is unset.

```


```diff
+        return err(e).into_response();

```

**FILE: `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`**

HUNKS:
```text
@@ -27,3 +27,23 @@ pub fn err(msg: impl ToString) -> impl IntoResponse {
```

AFTER / ADDED:
```diff
+
+/// 生产环境隐藏详细错误信息，开发环境显示详细信息
+pub fn safe_err(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> impl IntoResponse {
+    let env = std::env::var("ENVIRONMENT").unwrap_or_default();
+    let is_production = env == "production";
+
+    if is_production {
+        err("Internal server error".to_string()) // 统一为 String 类型
```

```diff
+    let env = std::env::var("ENVIRONMENT").unwrap_or_default();
```

### E2E FLOW #34 — PUT /console/api/tokens/&#123;token&#125;

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Token → PUT /console/api/tokens/{token}`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`, `crates/common/src/constants.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方输入
│    ├─ Entry: PUT /console/api/tokens/{token}
│    ├─ Method / Path / Query / Headers / Body
│    └─ DECISION: 请求到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层结束，应用代码不执行 → END
│         └─ YES → Axum Unified App
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ start_server()（启动时）
│    ├─ create_default_database()
│    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    ├─ create_app()
│    ├─ TcpListener::bind()
│    └─ axum::serve()
│
├─ create_app()
│    ├─ merge(api::routes(...))
│    ├─ merge(internal_app)
│    ├─ optional merge(liveview_router)
│    ├─ fallback_service(router_app)
│    └─ middleware: CORS / Trace / request-id
│
├─ DECISION: Management API route 命中?
│    ├─ NO → other route
│    └─ YES → protected_routes
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ merge(token::routes())
└─ auth_middleware wraps protected router
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ auth_middleware() → verify_jwt()
└─ DECISION: JWT valid?
     ├─ NO → HTTP 401 → END
     └─ YES → Claims → next
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/token.rs
│
├─ routes() matches PUT /console/api/tokens/{token}
├─ update_token()
├─ parse Path / Query / Json according to method
├─ validate token-specific fields / rotation / whitelist parameters
└─ CALL TokenService::update_status(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/token/src/lib.rs
│
├─ TokenService::update_status()
└─ CALL RouterTokenModel::update_status(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/token.rs
│
├─ RouterTokenModel::update_status()
├─ DB read/write + token state/rotation/quota fields
└─ DECISION: DB/token operation successful?
     ├─ NO → DatabaseError / not found / validation result
     └─ YES → RouterToken / bool / TokenRotationResult / ()
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/token/src/lib.rs
│
└─ return Result to API handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/token.rs
│
├─ map result to ok(...) / err(...)
└─ return HTTP JSON response
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ auth_middleware()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → verify_jwt() @ crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ verify_jwt()

│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
```

```text
├─ create_app()
│    ├─ merge(api::routes(...))
│    ├─ merge(internal_app)
│    ├─ optional merge(liveview_router)
│    ├─ fallback_service(router_app)
│    └─ middleware: CORS / Trace / request-id
│
├─ DECISION: protected User route 命中?
│    ├─ NO → other route
│    └─ YES → protected_routes
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ merge(user::routes())
└─ auth_middleware wraps protected router
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/auth.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ auth_middleware() → verify_jwt()
└─ DECISION: JWT valid?
     ├─ NO → HTTP 401 → END
     └─ YES → Claims inserted
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/user.rs
│
├─ routes() matches GET /console/api/user/check_username
├─ check_username()
├─ Path/Query/Json/Claims extraction

│
▼

├─ password/JWT/balance/recharge logic as applicable
└─ DB calls: UserDatabase::get_user_by_username
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
│
├─ UserDatabase read/write
├─ db.get_connection() / SQL execution / row mapping
└─ DECISION: persistence operation successful?
     ├─ NO → DatabaseError → UserServiceError
     └─ YES → user/balance/roles/recharge result
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ return domain result
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/user.rs
│
├─ map domain result to API response
└─ return HTTP JSON
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）

│    │    ├─ get_jwt_secret()

│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ UserService::is_username_available()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → UserDatabase::get_user_by_username() @ crates/database/crates/user/src/lib.rs
```

```text
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → Management API / protected route candidate
│         └─ NO  → other top-level/fallback route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ protected_routes composition
│    ├─ route registered under Management API
│    └─ auth_middleware() wraps protected router
│
├─ JWT authentication
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    └─ DECISION: Authorization starts with Bearer?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → verify_jwt(...)
│
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/billing.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ billing_summary_handler()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: read/query state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│
├─ BillingService::get_billing_summary_for_user(db, claims.sub, start, end)
└─ CALL burncloud_database_router::get_billing_summary_for_user(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ build user/time filtered billing aggregation
├─ aggregate per-model request/token/cost state
└─ DECISION: billing SQL succeeds?
     ├─ NO → DatabaseError → BillingService → err(...)
     └─ YES → BillingSummary → API handler
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/billing.rs
│    │    ├─ billing_summary_handler()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → BillingService::get_billing_summary_for_user() @ crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ BillingService::get_billing_summary_for_user()
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ ok()
│    │    ├─ err()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

```diff
+/// 生产环境隐藏详细错误信息，开发环境显示详细信息
```

```text
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ billing_summary_handler()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: read/query state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│
├─ billing_summary_inner()
├─ DECISION: BURNCLOUD_INTERNAL_SECRET configured?
│    ├─ YES → x-internal-secret must match or HTTP 401
│    └─ NO  → no extra internal-secret rejection
└─ BillingService::get_billing_summary(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│
└─ BillingService::get_billing_summary() → database router
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ aggregate billing state from router_logs
└─ return BillingSummary
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│    │    ├─ billing_summary_handler()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → billing_summary_inner() @ crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│    │    ├─ billing_summary_inner()
│    └─ FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ BillingService::get_billing_summary()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

### E2E FLOW #51 — GET /console/api/monitor/security/events

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Monitoring / Security → GET /console/api/monitor/security/events`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /console/api/monitor/security/events
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → Management API / protected route candidate
│         └─ NO  → other top-level/fallback route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ protected_routes composition
│    ├─ route registered under Management API
│    └─ auth_middleware() wraps protected router
│
├─ JWT authentication
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    └─ DECISION: Authorization starts with Bearer?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → verify_jwt(...)
│
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ security_events()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: read/query state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│
└─ RouterLogService::get(...) → RouterLogModel::get(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ read router_logs
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│
├─ summary: compute_security_score() + compute_sparkline()
└─ events: filter status >= 400 → log_to_risk_event() → paginate
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│    │    ├─ security_events()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → RouterLogService::get() @ crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ compute_security_score()
│    │    ├─ compute_sparkline()
│    │    ├─ log_to_risk_event()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│    │    ├─ RouterLogService::get()
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ err()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: POST /console/api/monitor/security/emergency-circuit-break
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → Management API / protected route candidate
│         └─ NO  → other top-level/fallback route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ protected_routes composition
│    ├─ route registered under Management API
│    └─ auth_middleware() wraps protected router
│
├─ JWT authentication
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    └─ DECISION: Authorization starts with Bearer?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → verify_jwt(...)
│
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ security_emergency_circuit_break()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: write/mutate state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│
├─ validate reason non-empty
├─ post_router_internal("/console/internal/circuit-breaker/trip-all", body)
├─ build http://127.0.0.1:{PORT} URL
└─ optional X-Internal-Secret header
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ circuit_breaker_trip_all_handler()
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ trip_all() mutates known breaker states
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│    │    ├─ security_emergency_circuit_break()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → post_router_internal() @ crates/server/src/api/security.rs
│    │    ├─ post_router_internal()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ circuit_breaker_trip_all_handler()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → build_response_with_header() @ crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ build_response_with_header()
│    ├─ FILE: crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ CircuitBreaker::trip_all()
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ err()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

### E2E FLOW #57 — POST /console/api/cache/clear

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Cache → POST /console/api/cache/clear`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/response.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: POST /console/api/cache/clear
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → Management API / protected route candidate
│         └─ NO  → other top-level/fallback route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ protected_routes composition
│    ├─ route registered under Management API
│    └─ auth_middleware() wraps protected router
│
├─ JWT authentication
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    └─ DECISION: Authorization starts with Bearer?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → verify_jwt(...)
│
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/cache.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ clear()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: write/mutate state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/cache/src/service.rs
│
├─ DECISION: CacheService available / Redis connected?
│    ├─ NO → disabled/empty semantics or CacheError
│    └─ YES → ConnectionManager
├─ stats(): read Redis INFO/dbsize-like statistics
└─ clear_all(): KEYS "bc:*" → DECISION keys empty? → DEL matching keys
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/cache.rs
│    │    ├─ clear()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → ok() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    │    └─ CALL → err() @ crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    ├─ FILE: crates/service/crates/cache/src/service.rs
│    │    ├─ CacheService::stats()
│    │    ├─ CacheService::clear_all()
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/response.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ ok()
│    │    ├─ err()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

### E2E FLOW #60 — GET /console/api/usage/&#123;user_id&#125;

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Billing / Usage → GET /console/api/usage/{user_id}`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /console/api/usage/{user_id}
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → Management API / protected route candidate
│         └─ NO  → other top-level/fallback route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ protected_routes composition
│    ├─ route registered under Management API
│    └─ auth_middleware() wraps protected router
│
├─ JWT authentication
│    ├─ read Authorization header
│    └─ DECISION: Authorization starts with Bearer?
│         ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → verify_jwt(...)
│
├─ DECISION: JWT signature/claims valid?
│    ├─ NO  → HTTP 401 → END
│    └─ YES
│         ├─ Claims inserted into request extensions
│         └─ continue to route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ get_user_usage()
│
├─ Request extraction
│    ├─ Path params / Query params / JSON body as required by Method
│    ├─ authenticated Claims available from extensions
│    └─ DECISION: extraction/required fields valid?
│         ├─ NO  → client/error response → END
│         └─ YES → authorization/business checks
│
├─ Authorization + invariants
│    ├─ Route uses authenticated Claims/user context as implemented
│    ├─ validate ID/status/range/reason/etc. according to handler
│    └─ DECISION: authorization/invariants pass?
│         ├─ NO  → 4xx/error payload → END
│         └─ YES → service/database call
│
├─ Service / Database boundary
│    ├─ operation type: read/query state
│    ├─ invoke route-specific Service / Database method
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO  → map error → HTTP error response
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
├─ State effects
│    ├─ READ routes: no intended mutation beyond incidental telemetry
│    ├─ WRITE routes: persist create/update/delete/config action
│    └─ route-specific async/internal calls execute before/around result when implemented
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/router-log/src/lib.rs
│
└─ RouterLogService::get_usage_by_user(db, user_id)
│
▼
FILE: crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ SUM prompt_tokens / completion_tokens for user_id
└─ return tuple → handler calculates total_tokens
│
├─ Response mapping
│    ├─ domain model → DTO/JSON
│    ├─ pagination/summary fields where applicable
│    └─ serialize success payload
│
├─ HTTP exit
│    └─ return success or mapped error status/body
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/log.rs
│    │    ├─ get_user_usage()

│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件

END
```

```text
│

│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
```

```text
├─ Internal route match
│    └─ health_status_handler()
│
├─ Authentication boundary
│    ├─ Router internal_app itself is not wrapped by Management JWT middleware
│    └─ network exposure therefore depends on server deployment/binding/firewall
│
├─ Runtime-state operation
│    ├─ read/mutate in-memory Router runtime services

│         ├─ NO  → route-specific 5xx/timeout/error response
│         └─ YES → perform operation
│
├─ Route-specific state

│
├─ Serialize result
│    └─ DECISION: operation/result successful?
│         ├─ NO  → error HTTP response
│         └─ YES → JSON success response
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    └─ FILE: crates/router/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ health_status_handler()

│    │    ├─ build_response_with_header()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

```text
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → api::routes()
│         └─ NO → other route
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ OpenAPI routes are currently inside protected_routes
│    ├─ auth_middleware()
│    └─ DECISION: JWT valid?
│         ├─ NO → HTTP 401 → END
│         └─ YES → route handler
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│
├─ Handler
│    └─ openapi_json()
│
├─ Content construction
│    └─ DECISION: endpoint type?
│         ├─ /api-docs/openapi.json
│         │    ├─ construct OpenAPI 3.0.3 object
│         │    ├─ include documented paths/schemas
│         │    └─ serialize JSON
│         └─ /swagger-ui[/]
│              ├─ construct embedded Swagger HTML shell
│              └─ browser later loads Swagger UI assets
│
├─ Response
│    ├─ JSON endpoint → application/json
│    └─ UI endpoint → text/html
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    └─ FILE: crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│    │    ├─ openapi_json()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → generate_openapi_spec() @ crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│    │    ├─ generate_openapi_spec()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → get_version() @ crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → generate_paths() @ crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│    │    │    └─ CALL → generate_schemas() @ crates/server/src/api/openapi.rs
│    │    ├─ get_version()
│    │    ├─ generate_paths()
│    │    ├─ generate_schemas()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
```

### E2E FLOW #69 — burncloud

**树路径：** `CLI / Executables → burncloud → burncloud`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`

```text
START
│
├─ Shell / OS: burncloud（无参数）
│
▼
FILE: src/main.rs
│
├─ main()
│    ├─ dotenvy::dotenv()
│    ├─ ensure_master_key()
│    ├─ init_logging()
│    └─ match args == [binary]
│
├─ DECISION: target_os == windows?
│    ├─ YES
│    │    ├─ std::thread::spawn(background server thread)
│    │    │    ├─ tokio::runtime::Runtime::new()
│    │    │    ├─ HOST / PORT
│    │    │    └─ burncloud_server::start_server(host, port, false)
│    │    │         └─ enable_liveview=false：后台 Server 不挂 LiveView Router
│    │    └─ main thread → burncloud_client::launch_gui_with_tray()
│    │
│    └─ NO（Linux/macOS 等）
│         ├─ print Headless Mode startup line
│         └─ run_async_server()
│              └─ burncloud_server::start_server(host, port, true)
│
├─ Windows server thread 后续主链与 `burncloud server` 的 start_server/create_app/create_router_app 相同
├─ Non-Windows 后续主链与 `burncloud server` 完全相同
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs
│
├─ Windows GUI branch: launch_gui_with_tray()
│    ├─ WindowBuilder
│    ├─ Config::new().with_window(...).with_data_directory(...)
│    └─ LaunchBuilder::desktop().launch(AppWithTray)
│
├─ AppWithTray()
│    ├─ DesktopMode context
│    ├─ maximize window
│    ├─ std::thread::spawn(start_tray)
│    ├─ spawn async show-window poll loop
│    └─ render App()
│
└─ App()
     ├─ use_init_i18n()
     ├─ use_init_toast()
     ├─ use_init_auth()
     ├─ use_init_theme()
     └─ Router<Route>
│
▼
END / LONG-RUNNING SERVER + UI LOOPS
```

```text
END = server shutdown or fatal serve error
```

### E2E FLOW #71 — router

**树路径：** `CLI / Executables → burncloud → router`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/server/src/logging.rs`, `crates/database/src/database.rs`, `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/limiter.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/router/src/exchange_rate.rs`, `crates/router/src/channel_health_manager.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/server/src/api/auth.rs`

```text
│    ├─ monitor::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ user::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ security::security_routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ openapi::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ cache::routes() [REGISTER]
│    └─ auth_middleware layered over protected router [REGISTER]
│
├─ IMPORTANT: routes() 函数在启动期执行；具体 HTTP Handler 此刻不执行
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/lib.rs
│
├─ liveview_router(db)
│    ├─ LiveViewPool::new()
│    ├─ register HTML shell routes /, /home, /login, /register, ...
│    ├─ register /console and /console/*
│    ├─ register /favicon.ico
│    └─ register WS_PATH
│         └─ app::App is passed as future WebSocket-session callback [REGISTER ONLY]
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ create_app() returns Unified Router
├─ SocketAddr::parse(host:port)
├─ TcpListener::bind(addr).await
├─ axum::serve(listener, app).await
│
└─ LONG-RUNNING STATE
     ├─ HTTP listener waits for Management / Internal / Data Plane / LiveView traffic
     ├─ monitor updater keeps running
     ├─ exchange-rate task keeps running
     ├─ price-sync task keeps running
     ├─ AIMD feedback consumer keeps running
     ├─ RouterLog writer keeps running
     └─ RequestLog writer keeps running
│
▼
END = server shutdown or fatal serve error
```

```text
│

END / DESKTOP EVENT LOOP
```

```text
│         └─ YES → perform operation
│
├─ External/state I/O
│    ├─ DB / filesystem / HTTP / service operation depending on command
│    └─ DECISION: operation succeeds?
│         ├─ NO → print/return error → END
│         └─ YES → domain result
│
```

### E2E FLOW #83 — System Monitor Auto Update

**树路径：** `Background Jobs / Async Side Effects → Long-running Jobs → System Monitor Auto Update`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/service/crates/monitor/src/collectors/cpu.rs`, `crates/service/crates/monitor/src/collectors/memory.rs`, `crates/service/crates/monitor/src/collectors/disk.rs`, `crates/database/src/database.rs`, `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/service/crates/user/src/lib.rs`, `crates/router/src/limiter.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/router/src/exchange_rate.rs`

```text
START / TRIGGER
│
├─ Trigger source
│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message
│    └─ task: System Monitor Auto Update
│
├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│
├─ Core job operation
│    ├─ execute System Monitor Auto Update
│    └─ may call DB / HTTP / filesystem / runtime service depending on task
│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
│
```

### E2E FLOW #85 — Exchange Rate Sync

**树路径：** `Background Jobs / Async Side Effects → Long-running Jobs → Exchange Rate Sync`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`

```text
START / TRIGGER
│
├─ Trigger source
│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message
│    └─ task: Exchange Rate Sync
│
├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│
├─ Core job operation
│    ├─ execute Exchange Rate Sync
│    └─ may call DB / HTTP / filesystem / runtime service depending on task
│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
```

### E2E FLOW #87 — Async Router Log Writer

**树路径：** `Background Jobs / Async Side Effects → Long-running Jobs → Async Router Log Writer`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/router/src/lib.rs`, `crates/database/crates/router/src/log.rs`, `crates/router/src/limiter.rs`, `crates/router/src/circuit_breaker.rs`, `crates/router/src/exchange_rate.rs`, `crates/router/src/channel_health_manager.rs`

```text
START / TRIGGER
│
├─ Trigger source
│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message
│    └─ task: Async Router Log Writer
│
├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│
├─ Core job operation
│    ├─ execute Async Router Log Writer
│    └─ may call DB / HTTP / filesystem / runtime service depending on task
│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
```

**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`

```text
│

│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message

├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│

│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
```
